# Reviewed symbol comparisons

Generated from [review.json](review.json). See [scope and confidence](README.md).

620 main-ELF entries; 2 concept-only; 3 name-candidate; 378 related-different; 237 supported-role.

`supported-role` supports the stated API role, not complete semantic equivalence.

| [0x1401A8](../SYMBOLS_CRI_SJUNI_LINKED_CHUNK_POOLS.md) | [SJUNI_Destroy](https://github.com/AshfordFamily/recvx-decomp/blob/657b0c0c9489943116c71f151804ad7304fca975/src/cri/mwlib/ee/lib/libadxe/sj_uni.c#L75-L87) | supported-role | Clear the object, retaining caller ownership of nodes/payload storage; no freeing of borrowed buffers. |
| [0x1401F8](../SYMBOLS_CRI_SJUNI_LINKED_CHUNK_POOLS.md) | [SJUNI_Reset](https://github.com/AshfordFamily/recvx-decomp/blob/657b0c0c9489943116c71f151804ad7304fca975/src/cri/mwlib/ee/lib/libadxe/sj_uni.c#L108-L139) | supported-role | Both relink the external node free list and clear four heads. | [0x140288](../SYMBOLS_CRI_SJUNI_LINKED_CHUNK_POOLS.md) | [SJUNI_GetNumData](https://github.com/AshfordFamily/recvx-decomp/blob/657b0c0c9489943116c71f151804ad7304fca975/src/cri/mwlib/ee/lib/libadxe/sj_uni.c#L141-L167) | supported-role | Sum payload lengths for a selected queue; this returns bytes, not node count. |
| [0x140300](../SYMBOLS_CRI_SJUNI_LINKED_CHUNK_POOLS.md) | [SJUNI_GetChunk](https://github.com/AshfordFamily/recvx-decomp/blob/657b0c0c9489943116c71f151804ad7304fca975/src/cri/mwlib/ee/lib/libadxe/sj_uni.c#L169-L232) | supported-role | Whole-front-span removal returns its node to the free list; a smaller request only splits in mode 1. | [0x140438](../SYMBOLS_CRI_SJUNI_LINKED_CHUNK_POOLS.md) | [SJUNI_PutChunk](https://github.com/AshfordFamily/recvx-decomp/blob/657b0c0c9489943116c71f151804ad7304fca975/src/cri/mwlib/ee/lib/libadxe/sj_uni.c#L234-L299) | supported-role | Append or merge a physically adjacent tail in mode 1; exhausted node pool reports an error.
